# Metzudat Zion on Psalms

מצודת ציון על תהילים

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20Zion%20on%20Psalms](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Metzudat%20Zion%20on%20Psalms)

ביאור למילים מקראיות, במקור פורסם עם מצודת דוד כחיבור משולב.

Work originally composed together with the Metzudat David as one work, explaining individual words.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Acharonim on Tanakh/Metzudat Zion/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): On Your Way (Segments: 2027, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Sefaria Community Translation (Segments: 2)
